# t_cluster - t_veto method scan

Generated by `tcluster_minus_tveto_scan.py`. One split per sample; fit: 0.25 ns bins, Gauss + constant within +-5 ns of the mode; robust sigma = half the 16-84 % range of the hits within +-5 ns of the mode (truncated, so it understates widths above ~2 ns); core sigma = iterative +-2 sigma Gaussian, no constant, 0.2 ns bins. BEST = smallest sigma_fit per (sample, veto) among rows with >= 100 hits in +-5 ns, err < 30 % of sigma, not the unaligned pooled row. `walk(in-sample)` variants fit the correction on the same hits, so they are optimistic. Cluster time: MF/CFD = seed matched-filter / WaveAnalyzer CFD time, cal/raw = with/without the time_calib.json offset, wm = energy-weighted mean over members with amplitude >= 50 ADC. Veto time: LI = largest-integral pulse; gate = within +-10 ns of the first-pass mode; h50/h100 = pulse height cut; int2000 = integral > 2000; lnfit = log-normal-fit CFD (time_algo 2) (only if the veto-fit pass was run). Vall = largest integral over V1-V4, with (Vall) or without (Vall_noalign) per-counter alignment.
